Theorem 216.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let the detection structure S satisfy the conditions of convergence analysis framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 394. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 416 and converges at rate O(1/n^4).

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to detection theory, we decompose the problem into 394 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 416. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(4/n). QED.

Theorem 216.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: For any detection module M satisfying the structural decomposition hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 238. The projective resolution has length at most 190 with Betti numbers bounded by 4.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 238 is immediate from the structural decomposition property. For the inductive step, we decompose the detection structure using the structural decomposition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 190. Each subproblem has a solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 4. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
